FILE: app/src/main/java/com/sai/decisiongraveyard/viewmodel/DecisionDetailViewModel.java
====================================================================================================

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.viewmodel;

import android.app.Application;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel;
import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData;

import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.DecisionRecord;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.DecisionRepository;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.RepositoryProvider;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class DecisionDetailViewModel extends AndroidViewModel {

    public static class ActionState {
        public final boolean saved;
        public final boolean deleted;
        public final String message;

        public ActionState(boolean saved, boolean deleted, String message) {
            this.saved = saved;
            this.deleted = deleted;
            this.message = message;
        }
    }

    private final DecisionRepository repository;
    private final ExecutorService executorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    private final MutableLiveData<DecisionRecord> decisionRecord = new MutableLiveData<>();
    private final MutableLiveData<ActionState> actionState = new MutableLiveData<>();

    public DecisionDetailViewModel(@NonNull Application application) {
        super(application);
        repository = RepositoryProvider.getInstance(application).getDecisionRepository();
    }

    public LiveData<DecisionRecord> getDecisionRecord() {
        return decisionRecord;
    }

    public LiveData<ActionState> getActionState() {
        return actionState;
    }

    public void loadDecision(long decisionId) {
        executorService.execute(() -> {
            try {
                decisionRecord.postValue(repository.getDecisionRecord(decisionId));
            } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
                actionState.postValue(new ActionState(
                        false,
                        false,
                        exception.getMessage() == null
                                ? "Couldn't load this decision."
                                : exception.getMessage()
                ));
            }
        });
    }

    public void saveEvaluation(long decisionId, String outcome, String reflectionNotes) {
        repository.saveEvaluation(decisionId, outcome, reflectionNotes, new DecisionRepository.ActionCallback() {
            @Override
            public void onSuccess() {
                actionState.postValue(new ActionState(true, false, "Evaluation saved."));
            }

            @Override
            public void onError(String error) {
                actionState.postValue(new ActionState(
                        false,
                        false,
                        error != null ? error : "Couldn't save the evaluation."
                ));
            }
        });
    }

    public void deleteDecision(long decisionId) {
        executorService.execute(() -> {
            try {
                repository.deleteDecision(decisionId);
                actionState.postValue(new ActionState(false, true, "Decision deleted."));
            } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
                actionState.postValue(new ActionState(
                        false,
                        false,
                        exception.getMessage() == null
                                ? "Couldn't delete the decision."
                                : exception.getMessage()
                ));
            }
        });
    }

    public void clearActionState() {
        actionState.setValue(null);
    }

    @Override
    protected void onCleared() {
        super.onCleared();
        executorService.shutdownNow();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/sai/decisiongraveyard/viewmodel/HomeViewModel.java
====================================================================================================

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.viewmodel;

import android.app.Application;
import android.util.Log;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel;
import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData;

import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.DecisionRecord;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.DecisionRepository;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.RepositoryProvider;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class HomeViewModel extends AndroidViewModel {

    private static final String TAG = "HomeViewModel";

    public static class HomeScreenState {
        public final List<DecisionRecord> filteredRecords;
        public final int totalCount;
        public final int readyCount;
        public final int upcomingCount;
        public final String identityLabel;
        public final int qualityScore;

        public HomeScreenState(List<DecisionRecord> filteredRecords, int totalCount,
                               int readyCount, int upcomingCount, String identityLabel, int qualityScore) {
            this.filteredRecords = filteredRecords;
            this.totalCount = totalCount;
            this.readyCount = readyCount;
            this.upcomingCount = upcomingCount;
            this.identityLabel = identityLabel;
            this.qualityScore = qualityScore;
        }
    }

    private final DecisionRepository repository;
    private final ExecutorService executorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    private final MutableLiveData<HomeScreenState> screenState = new MutableLiveData<>();

    private String statusFilter = "all";
    private String categoryFilter = "All";
    private List<DecisionRecord> cachedRecords = new ArrayList<>();
    private ListenerRegistration registration;

    public HomeViewModel(@NonNull Application application) {
        super(application);
        repository = RepositoryProvider.getInstance(application).getDecisionRepository();
        startListening();
    }

    private void startListening() {
        if (registration != null) {
            registration.remove();
        }
        registration = repository.listenToDecisions(new DecisionRepository.DecisionRecordsListener() {
            @Override
            public void onChanged(@NonNull List<DecisionRecord> records) {
                Log.d(TAG, "Decisions updated: " + records.size() + " items");
                cachedRecords = records;
                postFilteredState();
            }

            @Override
            public void onError(@NonNull String message) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Error listening to decisions: " + message);
            }
        });
    }

    public LiveData<HomeScreenState> getScreenState() {
        return screenState;
    }

    public void refresh() {
        if (registration == null) {
            startListening();
        }
    }

    public void setStatusFilter(String statusFilter) {
        this.statusFilter = statusFilter;
        postFilteredState();
    }

    public void setCategoryFilter(String categoryFilter) {
        this.categoryFilter = categoryFilter;
        postFilteredState();
    }

    private void postFilteredState() {
        List<DecisionRecord> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
        int readyCount = 0;
        int upcomingCount = 0;
        int goodCount = 0;
        int totalEvaluated = 0;

        for (DecisionRecord record : cachedRecords) {
            if (record.isReadyForReview()) {
                readyCount++;
            } else if (record.isUpcoming()) {
                upcomingCount++;
            }

            if (record.isEvaluated()) {
                totalEvaluated++;
                if ("good".equals(record.getEvaluation().getOutcome())) {
                    goodCount++;
                }
            }

            if (!matchesStatus(record) || !matchesCategory(record)) {
                continue;
            }
            filtered.add(record);
        }

        // Calculate identity label and quality score
        int qualityScore = totalEvaluated == 0 ? 0 : (int) Math.round(goodCount * 100.0 / totalEvaluated);
        String identityLabel = calculateIdentityLabel(qualityScore);

        Log.d(TAG, "Posting screen state: filteredCount=" + filtered.size() + ", totalCount=" + cachedRecords.size());
        screenState.postValue(new HomeScreenState(filtered, cachedRecords.size(), readyCount, upcomingCount, identityLabel, qualityScore));
    }

    private String calculateIdentityLabel(int score) {
        if (score >= 80) return "Level 4: Disciplined";
        if (score >= 60) return "Level 3: Controlled";
        if (score >= 40) return "Level 2: Aware";
        return "Level 1: Chaotic";
    }

    private boolean matchesStatus(DecisionRecord record) {
        if ("ready".equals(statusFilter)) {
            return record.isReadyForReview();
        }
        if ("reviewed".equals(statusFilter)) {
            return record.isEvaluated();
        }
        return true;
    }

    private boolean matchesCategory(DecisionRecord record) {
        return "All".equals(categoryFilter)
                || categoryFilter.equalsIgnoreCase(record.getDecision().getCategory());
    }

    @Override
    protected void onCleared() {
        super.onCleared();
        if (registration != null) {
            registration.remove();
        }
        executorService.shutdownNow();
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/sai/decisiongraveyard/viewmodel/InsightsViewModel.java
====================================================================================================

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.viewmodel;

import android.app.Application;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel;
import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData;

import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.AnalyticsSnapshot;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.DecisionRecord;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.DecisionRepository;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.RepositoryProvider;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class InsightsViewModel extends AndroidViewModel {

    private final DecisionRepository repository;
    private final ExecutorService executorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    private final MutableLiveData<AnalyticsSnapshot> analyticsSnapshot = new MutableLiveData<>();
    private final MutableLiveData<Integer> streakCount = new MutableLiveData<>(0);
    private final MutableLiveData<Integer> badStreakCount = new MutableLiveData<>(0);
    private final MutableLiveData<Boolean> brutalMode = new MutableLiveData<>(false);

    public InsightsViewModel(@NonNull Application application) {
        super(application);
        repository = RepositoryProvider.getInstance(application).getDecisionRepository();
    }

    public LiveData<AnalyticsSnapshot> getAnalyticsSnapshot() {
        return analyticsSnapshot;
    }

    public LiveData<Integer> getStreakCount() {
        return streakCount;
    }

    public LiveData<Integer> getBadStreakCount() {
        return badStreakCount;
    }

    public LiveData<Boolean> getBrutalMode() {
        return brutalMode;
    }

    public void setBrutalMode(boolean enabled) {
        brutalMode.setValue(enabled);
        refresh();
    }

    public void refresh() {
        executorService.execute(() -> {
            try {
                Boolean isBrutal = brutalMode.getValue();
                analyticsSnapshot.postValue(repository.getAnalyticsSnapshot(isBrutal != null && isBrutal));
                streakCount.postValue(calculateStreak());
                badStreakCount.postValue(calculateBadStreak());
            } catch (RuntimeException ignored) {
                analyticsSnapshot.postValue(new AnalyticsSnapshot(
                        0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                        java.util.Collections.emptyList(),
                        java.util.Collections.emptyList()
                ));
                streakCount.postValue(0);
                badStreakCount.postValue(0);
            }
        });
    }

    private int calculateStreak() {
        List<DecisionRecord> records = repository.getAllDecisionRecords();
        int streak = 0;
        long lastEvaluatedTime = 0;

        // Sort by evaluation time ascending (oldest first)
        records.sort((a, b) -> {
            Long timeA = a.isEvaluated() ? a.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt() : Long.MAX_VALUE;
            Long timeB = b.isEvaluated() ? b.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt() : Long.MAX_VALUE;
            return timeA.compareTo(timeB);
        });

        for (DecisionRecord record : records) {
            if (!record.isEvaluated()) continue;

            String outcome = record.getEvaluation().getOutcome();
            long evalTime = record.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt();

            if ("good".equals(outcome)) {
                // Check if consecutive (within 24 hours of last good decision)
                if (lastEvaluatedTime == 0 || Math.abs(evalTime - lastEvaluatedTime) <= 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    streak++;
                    lastEvaluatedTime = evalTime;
                } else if (evalTime > lastEvaluatedTime) {
                    // This is a newer decision, continue streak
                    streak++;
                    lastEvaluatedTime = evalTime;
                } else {
                    break; // Streak broken (gap too large)
                }
            } else {
                break; // Streak broken by bad/neutral decision
            }
        }

        return streak;
    }

    private int calculateBadStreak() {
        List<DecisionRecord> records = repository.getAllDecisionRecords();
        int badStreak = 0;
        long lastEvaluatedTime = 0;

        // Sort by evaluation time ascending (oldest first)
        records.sort((a, b) -> {
            Long timeA = a.isEvaluated() ? a.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt() : Long.MAX_VALUE;
            Long timeB = b.isEvaluated() ? b.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt() : Long.MAX_VALUE;
            return timeA.compareTo(timeB);
        });

        for (DecisionRecord record : records) {
            if (!record.isEvaluated()) continue;

            String outcome = record.getEvaluation().getOutcome();
            long evalTime = record.getEvaluation().getEvaluatedAt();

            if ("bad".equals(outcome)) {
                // Check if consecutive (within 24 hours of last bad decision)
                if (lastEvaluatedTime == 0 || Math.abs(evalTime - lastEvaluatedTime) <= 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
                    badStreak++;
                    lastEvaluatedTime = evalTime;
                } else if (evalTime > lastEvaluatedTime) {
                    // This is a newer decision, continue streak
                    badStreak++;
                    lastEvaluatedTime = evalTime;
                } else {
                    break; // Streak broken (gap too large)
                }
            } else {
                break; // Streak broken by good/neutral decision
            }
        }

        return badStreak;
    }

    @Override
    protected void onCleared() {
        super.onCleared();
        executorService.shutdownNow();
    }
}

FILE: app/src/test/java/com/sai/decisiongraveyard/repository/DecisionRepositoryTest.java

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository;

import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.Decision;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.DecisionRecord;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.model.Evaluation;

import org.junit.Before;
import org.junit.Test;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.MockitoAnnotations;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class DecisionRepositoryTest {

    @Mock
    private DecisionRepository repository;

    @Before
    public void setUp() {
        MockitoAnnotations.openMocks(this);
    }

    @Test
    public void checkDecisionWarning_returnsWarning_whenRegretRateIsHigh() {
        // Arrange
        String category = "money";
        int hour = 10;
        DecisionRepository.DecisionWarning expectedWarning = new DecisionRepository.DecisionWarning(
                true, "Warning message", 75, new ArrayList<>()
        );
        
        when(repository.checkDecisionWarning(category, hour)).thenReturn(expectedWarning);

        // Act
        DecisionRepository.DecisionWarning actualWarning = repository.checkDecisionWarning(category, hour);

        // Assert
        assertNotNull(actualWarning);
        assertTrue(actualWarning.shouldWarn);
        assertEquals(75, actualWarning.regretRate);
    }

    @Test
    public void hasSimilarDecision_returnsTrue_whenDecisionExists() {
        // Arrange
        String title = "Buy stocks";
        String category = "money";
        long time = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        when(repository.hasSimilarDecision(title, category, time)).thenReturn(true);

        // Act
        boolean exists = repository.hasSimilarDecision(title, category, time);

        // Assert
        assertTrue(exists);
    }
}

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.util;

import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.Calendar;

public class DateUtilsTest {

    @Test
    public void getCategoryDisplayName_returnsCorrectValues() {
        assertEquals("MONEY", DateUtils.getCategoryDisplayName("money"));
        assertEquals("HEALTH", DateUtils.getCategoryDisplayName("health"));
        assertEquals("LOVE", DateUtils.getCategoryDisplayName("relationship"));
        assertEquals("PERSONAL", DateUtils.getCategoryDisplayName(null));
        assertEquals("UNKNOWN", DateUtils.getCategoryDisplayName("unknown"));
    }

    @Test
    public void getHourLabel_returnsCorrectLabels() {
        assertEquals("Morning", DateUtils.getHourLabel(9));
        assertEquals("Afternoon", DateUtils.getHourLabel(14));
        assertEquals("Evening", DateUtils.getHourLabel(19));
        assertEquals("Late night", DateUtils.getHourLabel(2));
        assertEquals("Late night", DateUtils.getHourLabel(23));
    }

    @Test
    public void formatCountdown_returnsFormattedStrings() {
        long oneDay = 24L * 60 * 60 * 1000;
        long oneHour = 60L * 60 * 1000;
        long oneMinute = 60L * 1000;

        assertEquals("1d", DateUtils.formatCountdown(oneDay));
        assertEquals("1h", DateUtils.formatCountdown(oneHour));
        assertEquals("1m", DateUtils.formatCountdown(oneMinute));
        assertEquals("<1m", DateUtils.formatCountdown(30 * 1000));
    }
}

package com.sai.decisiongraveyard.viewmodel;

import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

import android.app.Application;

import androidx.arch.core.executor.testing.InstantTaskExecutorRule;
import androidx.lifecycle.Observer;

import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.DecisionRepository;
import com.sai.decisiongraveyard.repository.RepositoryProvider;

import org.junit.Before;
import org.junit.Rule;
import org.junit.Test;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.MockitoAnnotations;

public class AddDecisionViewModelTest {

    @Rule
    public InstantTaskExecutorRule instantTaskExecutorRule = new InstantTaskExecutorRule();

    @Mock
    private Application application;
    @Mock
    private DecisionRepository repository;
    @Mock
    private RepositoryProvider repositoryProvider;

    private AddDecisionViewModel viewModel;

    @Before
    public void setUp() {
        MockitoAnnotations.openMocks(this);
        
        // Mock the RepositoryProvider to return our mock repository
        // Note: This assumes RepositoryProvider can be mocked or injected. 
        // In the real code, it uses a singleton. For unit tests, we usually use a testing version.
        viewModel = new AddDecisionViewModel(application);
        // Since we can't easily swap the repository in the current constructor, 
        // a real test would use a Factory or Dependency Injection.
    }

    @Test
    public void saveDecision_withEmptyTitle_updatesSaveStateWithError() {
        // Arrange
        Observer<AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState> observer = mock(Observer.class);
        viewModel.getSaveState().observeForever(observer);

        // Act
        viewModel.saveDecision("", "Description", "money", System.currentTimeMillis() + 100000);

        // Assert
        ArgumentCaptor<AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState.class);
        verify(observer).onChanged(captor.capture());
        assertFalse(captor.getValue().success);
        assertEquals("Title is required.", captor.getValue().message);
    }

    @Test
    public void saveDecision_withPastDate_updatesSaveStateWithError() {
        // Arrange
        Observer<AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState> observer = mock(Observer.class);
        viewModel.getSaveState().observeForever(observer);

        // Act
        viewModel.saveDecision("Title", "Description", "money", System.currentTimeMillis() - 1000);

        // Assert
        ArgumentCaptor<AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AddDecisionViewModel.SaveState.class);
        verify(observer).onChanged(captor.capture());
        assertFalse(captor.getValue().success);
        assertEquals("Choose a future review date.", captor.getValue().message);
    }
}

# DecisionGraveyard - Project Documentation

## Table of Contents
1. [Project Overview](#project-overview)
2. [Architecture](#architecture)
3. [Technology Stack](#technology-stack)
4. [Project Structure](#project-structure)
5. [Data Layer](#data-layer)
6. [Repository Layer](#repository-layer)
7. [ViewModel Layer](#viewmodel-layer)
8. [UI Layer](#ui-layer)
9. [Firebase Integration](#firebase-integration)
10. [Notification System](#notification-system)
11. [Utilities](#utilities)
12. [Key Flows](#key-flows)
13. [Design Patterns](#design-patterns)

---

## Project Overview

**DecisionGraveyard** is an Android application that helps users track their decisions over time and evaluate their outcomes later. The core concept is that users "bury" decisions with a scheduled review date, then later evaluate whether the decision was good, bad, or neutral. This creates a feedback loop for decision-making patterns.

### Key Features
- **Decision Tracking**: Log decisions with title, description, category, and scheduled review date
- **Delayed Evaluation**: Decisions are "locked" until the review date
- **Outcome Assessment**: Evaluate decisions as good, bad, or neutral with reflection notes
- **Analytics**: View decision quality scores, category-based regret rates, and time-of-day patterns
- **Risk Assessment**: Visual feedback based on historical decision patterns
- **Notifications**: Reminders when decisions are ready for review
- **Export**: Export decision history to CSV
- **Theme Support**: Dark and light mode
- **Google Sign-In**: Authenticate with Google account for seamless login
- **Activity Tracking**: Schedule and track activities with completion status
- **Pattern Detection**: Automatic detection of missed activity patterns
- **Recovery Missions**: Structured missions to break negative patterns
- **AI-Powered Insights**: Personalized insights generated by Google Gemini API
- **Pre-Miss Reminders**: Notifications 10 minutes before activity deadlines
- **WorkManager Integration**: Background periodic checks for pattern analysis

---

## Architecture

The project follows **MVVM (Model-View-ViewModel)** architecture with a clean separation of concerns:

```
┌─────────────────┐
│     UI Layer    │
│  (Activities/   │
│   Fragments)    │
└────────┬────────┘
         │ LiveData
┌────────▼────────┐
│ ViewModel Layer │
│  (Business      │
│   Logic)        │
└────────┬────────┘
         │
┌────────▼────────┐
│  Repository     │
│  (Data Access)  │
└────────┬────────┘
         │
┌────────▼────────┐
│  Data Layer     │
│  (Firebase      │
│   Firestore)    │
└─────────────────┘
```

### Architecture Principles
- **Unidirectional Data Flow**: User input → ViewModel → Repository → Data → Repository → ViewModel → UI
- **Reactive Updates**: LiveData and Firestore snapshot listeners for real-time updates
- **Separation of Concerns**: Each layer has a single responsibility
- **Repository Pattern**: Centralized data access abstraction

---

## Data Layer

### Models

#### Decision.java
Core decision entity representing a logged decision.

**Properties**:
- `id` (long): Unique identifier
- `title` (String): Decision title
- `description` (String): Detailed description
- `category` (String): Category (money, health, study, personal, work, relationship)
- `decisionTime` (long): When decision was made (timestamp)
- `evaluationTime` (long): When evaluation is scheduled (timestamp)
- `decisionHour` (int): Hour of day (0-23)
- `userId` (String): Firebase user ID
- `outcome` (String): Evaluation result (good/bad/neutral)
- `reflectionNotes` (String): User's reflection notes
- `evaluatedAt` (Long): Timestamp of evaluation

**Key Methods**:
- Multiple constructors for different use cases
- Standard getters/setters for all properties
- `toString()`: String representation for debugging

#### Evaluation.java
Stores evaluation results for decisions.

**Properties**:
- `id` (long): Unique identifier
- `decisionId` (long): Reference to decision
- `outcome` (String): good/bad/neutral
- `reflectionNotes` (String): User's thoughts
- `evaluatedAt` (long): Timestamp of evaluation

#### DecisionRecord.java
Combines Decision and Evaluation into a single object.

**Properties**:
- `decision` (Decision): The decision object
- `evaluation` (Evaluation): The evaluation object (nullable if not evaluated)

**Key Methods**:
- `isEvaluated()`: Returns true if evaluation exists
- `isReadyForReview()`: Returns true if current time >= evaluation time
- `isUpcoming()`: Returns true if not evaluated and not ready
- `getOutcomeOrPending()`: Returns outcome or "pending"

#### User.java
Firebase user document stored in Firestore.

**Properties**:
- `userId` (String): Firebase auth UID
- `email` (String): User email
- `createdAt` (Timestamp): Account creation timestamp

#### CategoryInsight.java
Analytics data for decision categories.

**Properties**:
- `category` (String): Category name
- `evaluatedCount` (int): Total evaluated decisions in category
- `regretCount` (int): Number of bad decisions
- `regretRate` (int): Percentage of regrets (0-100)

#### AnalyticsSnapshot.java
Comprehensive analytics snapshot.

**Properties**:
- `totalDecisions` (int): Total number of decisions
- `totalEvaluated` (int): Number of evaluated decisions
- `goodCount` (int): Number of good decisions
- `badCount` (int): Number of bad decisions
- `neutralCount` (int): Number of neutral decisions
- `readyForReviewCount` (int): Decisions ready for review
- `upcomingCount` (int): Decisions not yet ready
- `categoryInsights` (List<CategoryInsight>): Category-based analytics
- `generatedInsights` (List<String>): Textual insights

**Key Methods**:
- `isEmpty()`: Returns true if no evaluations exist
- `getGoodPercent()`: Percentage of good decisions
- `getBadPercent()`: Percentage of bad decisions
- `getNeutralPercent()`: Percentage of neutral decisions
- `getQualityScore()`: Overall decision quality score (0-100)

#### Activity.java
Activity tracking entity for scheduled tasks and commitments.

**Properties**:
- `activityId` (long): Unique identifier
- `title` (String): Activity title
- `category` (String): Category classification
- `scheduledTime` (long): When activity is scheduled (timestamp)
- `completed` (boolean): Completion status
- `completedTime` (Long): Timestamp when completed (nullable)
- `userId` (String): Firebase user ID
- `createdAt` (long): Creation timestamp

**Key Methods**:
- `getStatus()`: Returns status based on state
  - "completed": If marked as completed
  - "missed": If past scheduled time and not completed
  - "pending": If future time and not completed

#### PatternTracker.java
Analyzes activity completion patterns to detect failure trends.

**Properties**:
- `consecutiveMisses` (List<String>): List of consecutively missed activity titles
- `totalMisses` (int): Total missed activities in analysis period
- `mostMissedCategory` (String): Category with most misses
- `isPatternDetected` (boolean): Whether a concerning pattern exists
- `patternMessage` (String): Human-readable pattern description
- `recoveryActivitiesNeeded` (int): Number of activities to complete for recovery

**Key Methods**:
- Builder pattern for flexible construction
- Pattern detection logic (3+ consecutive misses triggers pattern)
- Recovery activity calculation

#### RecoveryMission.java
Represents a recovery mission to break negative patterns.

**Properties**:
- `missionId` (String): Unique mission identifier
- `title` (String): Mission title
- `description` (String): Mission description
- `activitiesToComplete` (int): Target activity count
- `activitiesCompleted` (int): Completed activity count
- `deadline` (long): Mission deadline timestamp
- `isCompleted` (boolean): Mission completion status
- `userId` (String): Firebase user ID

**Key Methods**:
- `getProgress()`: Returns completion percentage (0.0-1.0)
- `isExpired()`: Returns true if deadline passed and not completed
- Builder pattern for construction

### Database Layer

#### DatabaseHelper.java
SQLite database helper (legacy, Firebase Firestore is primary data store).

**Database Schema**:
```sql
CREATE TABLE decisions (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    category TEXT NOT NULL,
    decision_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
    evaluation_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
    decision_hour INTEGER NOT NULL
)

CREATE TABLE evaluations (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    decision_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    outcome TEXT NOT NULL,
    reflection_notes TEXT,
    evaluated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY(decision_id) REFERENCES decisions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
)
```

**Key Methods**:
- `insertDecision(Decision)`: Insert new decision
- `upsertEvaluation(Evaluation)`: Insert or update evaluation
- `deleteDecision(long)`: Delete decision (cascades to evaluations)
- `hasSimilarDecision(String, String, long)`: Check for duplicates in 24-hour window
- `getAllDecisionRecords()`: Get all decisions with evaluations
- `getDecisionRecord(long)`: Get single decision by ID
- `getPendingDecisions()`: Get unevaluated decisions

---

## Repository Layer

### DecisionRepository.java
Central data access layer using Firebase Firestore. Implements the Repository pattern to abstract data operations.

**Interfaces**:
```java
interface DecisionRecordsListener {
    void onChanged(@NonNull List<DecisionRecord> records);
    void onError(@NonNull String message);
}

interface ActionCallback {
    void onSuccess();
    void onError(String error);
}
```

**Key Methods**:

#### Decision Management
- `createDecision(long, String, String, String, long, ActionCallback)`: Async decision creation
  - Generates unique decision ID using ThreadLocalRandom
  - Validates user authentication
  - Creates Decision object with current timestamp
  - Saves to Firestore with user ID
  - Triggers data change notification via callback

- `createDecision(String, String, String, long)`: Blocking decision creation
  - Same logic as async version but blocks until complete
  - Returns generated decision ID

- `hasSimilarDecision(String, String, long)`: Check for similar decisions
  - Searches for decisions with same title and category within 24 hours
  - Case-insensitive comparison

- `deleteDecision(long)`: Remove decision from Firestore
  - Uses await() for synchronous deletion

- `getAllDecisionRecords()`: Fetch all user's decisions
  - Queries Firestore filtered by user ID
  - Maps documents to DecisionRecord objects
  - Sorts by decision time (descending)

- `getDecisionRecord(long)`: Fetch single decision by ID
  - Validates user ownership
  - Returns null if not found or belongs to different user

#### Evaluation Management
- `saveEvaluation(long, String, String)`: Blocking evaluation save
  - Updates decision document with outcome, reflection, timestamp
  - Uses Firestore update operation

- `saveEvaluation(long, String, String, ActionCallback)`: Async evaluation save
  - Same logic as blocking version with callback
  - Triggers data change notification

#### Real-time Updates
- `listenToDecisions(DecisionRecordsListener)`: Set up Firestore snapshot listener
  - Filters documents by user ID
  - Returns LiveData through callback interface
  - Handles errors gracefully without clearing data
  - Automatically updates on remote changes

- `getDataChangedTrigger()`: LiveData for manual refresh triggers
  - Used by other components to trigger refreshes

#### Analytics
- `getAnalyticsSnapshot()`: Calculate comprehensive analytics
  - Counts decisions by outcome (good/bad/neutral)
  - Calculates category-based regret rates
  - Analyzes time-of-day patterns (Late night, Morning, Afternoon, Evening)
  - Generates textual insights based on patterns
  - Identifies worst categories and time periods

  **Analytics Logic**:
  ```java
  // Category regret rate calculation
  regretRate = (regretCount / evaluatedCount) * 100
  
  // Time bucket analysis
  - Late night: 23:00 - 05:00
  - Morning: 05:00 - 12:00
  - Afternoon: 12:00 - 17:00
  - Evening: 17:00 - 23:00
  
  // Generated insights examples
  - "You regret money decisions 45% of the time."
  - "Decisions made after 11PM are mostly bad (60% regret)."
  - "You have 5 decisions ready for review right now."
  ```

#### Helper Methods
- `mapDecisionRecords(QuerySnapshot)`: Convert Firestore documents to DecisionRecord objects
  - Handles null userId edge cases
  - Parses document ID to long
  - Builds DecisionRecord from Decision and optional Evaluation
  - Sorts by decision time descending

- `buildDecisionRecord(Decision)`: Combine Decision with Evaluation
  - Checks if decision is evaluated (outcome not null/empty)
  - Creates Evaluation object from decision properties
  - Returns DecisionRecord

- `requireUserId()`: Validate authentication
  - Throws IllegalStateException if user not logged in

- `await(Task<T>)`: Synchronous task wrapper
  - Uses Tasks.await() to block on Firebase operations
  - Converts ExecutionException to RuntimeException
  - Handles InterruptedException

- `decisionDocument(long)`: Get Firestore document reference
  - Returns DocumentReference for decisions collection

### ActivityRepository.java
Central data access layer for activity tracking using Firebase Firestore.

**Interfaces**:
```java
interface ActivityListListener {
    void onChanged(@NonNull List<Activity> activities);
    void onError(@NonNull String message);
}

interface ActionCallback {
    void onSuccess();
    void onError(String error);
}
```

**Key Methods**:

#### Activity Management
- `createActivity(String, String, long, ActionCallback)`: Async activity creation
  - Generates unique activity ID using ThreadLocalRandom
  - Validates user authentication
  - Creates Activity object with current timestamp
  - Saves to Firestore with user ID
  - Schedules pre-miss reminder (10 minutes before scheduled time)
  - Triggers data change notification via callback

- `markAsCompleted(long, ActionCallback)`: Mark activity as completed
  - Updates Firestore document with completed=true and completedTime
  - Cancels pre-miss reminder
  - Triggers data change notification

- `deleteActivity(long, ActionCallback)`: Remove activity from Firestore
  - Uses Firestore delete operation
  - Cancels pre-miss reminder
  - Triggers data change notification

#### Activity Queries
- `getAllActivities()`: Fetch all user's activities
  - Queries Firestore filtered by user ID
  - Maps documents to Activity objects
  - Sorts by scheduled time (ascending)

- `getActivitiesForDay(long, long)`: Fetch activities for specific day
  - Queries by user ID and time range
  - Useful for daily activity views

- `getMissedActivities()`: Fetch missed activities
  - Queries for activities past scheduled time with completed=false
  - Used for pattern detection

#### Pattern Detection
- `detectFailurePattern()`: Analyze activity completion patterns
  - Analyzes activities from last 7 days
  - Identifies consecutive missed activities
  - Tracks most missed category
  - Detects concerning patterns (3+ consecutive misses)
  - Calculates recovery activities needed
  - Returns PatternTracker with analysis results

#### Real-time Updates
- `listenToActivities(ActivityListListener)`: Set up Firestore snapshot listener
  - Filters documents by user ID
  - Returns data through callback interface
  - Handles errors gracefully
  - Automatically updates on remote changes

- `getDataChangedTrigger()`: LiveData for manual refresh triggers

#### Helper Methods
- `getActivity(long)`: Fetch single activity by ID
  - Validates user ownership
  - Returns null if not found or belongs to different user

- `mapActivities(QuerySnapshot)`: Convert Firestore documents to Activity objects
  - Handles document ID parsing
  - Filters by user ID
  - Sorts by scheduled time ascending

- `requireUserId()`: Validate authentication
  - Throws IllegalStateException if user not logged in

- `await(Task<T>)`: Synchronous task wrapper
  - Uses Tasks.await() to block on Firebase operations
  - Converts ExecutionException to RuntimeException

### RecoveryMissionRepository.java
Central data access layer for recovery missions using Firebase Firestore.

**Key Methods**:
- `createRecoveryMission(RecoveryMission, ActionCallback)`: Create new recovery mission
  - Validates user authentication
  - Saves to Firestore with user ID
  - Triggers data change notification

- `updateMissionProgress(String, int, ActionCallback)`: Update mission completion count
  - Increments activitiesCompleted
  - Checks if mission is complete
  - Updates Firestore document

- `getActiveMissions()`: Fetch user's active recovery missions
  - Queries for missions with isCompleted=false
  - Filters by user ID

- `getMissionById(String)`: Fetch single mission by ID
  - Validates user ownership
  - Returns null if not found

### LLMService.java
AI-powered insights generation using Google Gemini API.

**Key Methods**:
- `generateInsights(String, boolean)`: Generate decision pattern insights
  - Takes decision data as string
  - Supports brutal mode (harsh, direct) or normal mode (analytical)
  - Returns CompletableFuture with generated insights
  - Falls back to null on error

- `generateActionableInsights(String)`: Generate actionable recommendations
  - Provides specific, practical recommendations
  - Max 15 words per recommendation
  - Returns as simple list

- `generateActivityInsights(String)`: Generate activity pattern insights
  - Analyzes missed activities and decision quality connection
  - Focuses on cause-effect relationships
  - Max 20 words per insight

**Prompt Engineering**:
- **Insight Prompt**: Analyzes decision patterns, categories, timing, and judgment quality
- **Actionable Prompt**: Focuses on practical, immediately implementable changes
- **Activity Prompt**: Examines how missed activities affect decision quality

**Error Handling**:
- Logs errors without crashing
- Returns null on failure (caller handles fallback)
- Uses fallback to pre-defined texts when API unavailable

**Configuration**:
- Uses GEMINI_API_KEY from BuildConfig
- Model: gemini-1.5-flash (fast, cost-effective)
- Executes on main thread via ContextCompat.getMainExecutor()

```java
class SaveState {
    boolean success;

    long decisionId;
    long evaluationTime;
    String title;
}
```

**Key Methods**:
- `saveDecision(String, String, String, long)`: Main save logic
  - Validates input (title required, category required, future evaluation time)
  - Generates unique decision ID
  - Checks for similar decisions asynchronously
  - Calls repository with callback
  - Updates LiveData with SaveState

- `setCustomDateMillis(Long)`, `clearCustomDate()`: Custom date picker support
- `clearSaveState()`: Reset save state

**Thread Management**: Uses ExecutorService for background operations

### DecisionDetailViewModel.java
Handles decision detail view and evaluation operations.

**State Classes**:
```java
class ActionState {
    boolean saved;
    boolean deleted;
    String message;
}
```

**Key Methods**:
- `loadDecision(long)`: Fetch decision by ID from repository
- `saveEvaluation(long, String, String)`: Save evaluation outcome and notes
- `deleteDecision(long)`: Remove decision from repository
- `clearActionState()`: Reset action state

### HomeViewModel.java
Manages home screen state with filtering and real-time updates.

**State Classes**:
```java
class HomeScreenState {
    List<DecisionRecord> filteredRecords;
    int totalCount;
    int readyCount;
    int upcomingCount;
}
```

**Key Methods**:
- `startListening()`: Set up Firestore real-time listener
  - Maintains ListenerRegistration for cleanup
  - Filters by user ID
  - Updates cached records on changes

- `setStatusFilter(String)`: Filter by status
  - "all": No filter
  - "ready": Only ready for review
  - "reviewed": Only evaluated

- `setCategoryFilter(String)`: Filter by category
  - "All" or specific category name

- `refresh()`: Restart listener if needed
- `postFilteredState()`: Apply filters and update screen state

**Filtering Logic**:
```java
boolean matchesStatus(DecisionRecord record) {
    if ("ready".equals(statusFilter)) return record.isReadyForReview();
    if ("reviewed".equals(statusFilter)) return record.isEvaluated();
    return true;
}

boolean matchesCategory(DecisionRecord record) {
    return "All".equals(categoryFilter) || 
           categoryFilter.equalsIgnoreCase(record.getDecision().getCategory());
}
```

### InsightsViewModel.java
Calculates analytics and insights for decision patterns.

**Key Methods**:
- `refresh()`: Calculate analytics snapshot and streak
  - Calls repository for analytics data
  - Calculates streak of consecutive good decisions

- `calculateStreak()`: Calculate consecutive good decisions
  - Sorts records by evaluation time (oldest first)
  - Counts consecutive good decisions within 24 hours
  - Breaks on bad/neutral decisions or large gaps
  - Returns streak count

```java
// Placeholder regret rates (would query actual data in production)
money: 45%
health: 25%
study: 30%
personal: 55%

relationship: 60%

// Risk levels
Low (<30%): Green, minimal visuals
```

## Firebase Integration

### AuthHelper.java
Authentication wrapper providing user-friendly error messages and user document management.

**Key Methods**:
- `login(String, String, AuthCallback)`: Sign in with email/password
  - Calls Firebase Authentication
  - On success: Creates/updates user document in Firestore
  - On error: Maps Firebase error to user-friendly message

- `register(String, String, AuthCallback)`: Create new account
  - Calls Firebase Authentication
  - On success: Creates user document in Firestore
  - On error: Maps Firebase error to user-friendly message

- `signInWithGoogle(GoogleSignInAccount, AuthCallback)`: Sign in with Google
  - Converts Google ID token to Firebase Auth credential
  - Authenticates with Firebase using Google credential
  - On success: Creates/updates user document in Firestore
  - On error: Maps Firebase error to user-friendly message

- `logout()`: Sign out current user
- `getCurrentUser()`: Get current FirebaseUser
- `isUserLoggedIn()`: Check authentication state

- `upsertUserDocument(FirebaseUser, boolean, AuthCallback)`: Create/update user in Firestore
  - Checks if user document exists
  - Sets createdAt timestamp for new users
  - Merges data for existing users
  - Signs out on failure if specified

- `mapAuthError(Exception)`: Convert Firebase auth errors to user-friendly messages
  - Weak password: "Password must be at least 6 characters."
  - User collision: "An account already exists with this email."
  - Network error: "Network error. Check your connection and try again."
  - Invalid email: "Enter a valid email address."
  - Wrong password: "Incorrect email or password."
  - User not found: "No account found for this email."

- `mapFirestoreError(Exception)`: Convert Firestore errors to user-friendly messages
  - Network error: "Network error. Check your connection and try again."
  - Generic: "We couldn't finish setting up your account."

**Error Handling**:
- Catches specific Firebase exception types
- Provides clear, actionable error messages
- Handles network errors gracefully
- Validates email format and password strength

## Notification System

### NotificationScheduler.java
Manages reminder notifications using Android AlarmManager.

**Key Methods**:
- `createNotificationChannel(Context)`: Setup Android O+ notification channel
  - Channel ID: "decision_review_channel"
  - Importance: DEFAULT
  - Only runs on Android O+

- `scheduleReminder(Context, long, String, long)`: Schedule alarm for evaluation time
  - Uses AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP
  - Allows while idle (setAndAllowWhileIdle)
  - Ensures trigger time is in future
  - Creates PendingIntent for broadcast

- `cancelReminder(Context, long)`: Cancel scheduled alarm
  - Uses AlarmManager.cancel()
  - Cancels PendingIntent with same request code

- `buildPendingIntent(Context, long, String)`: Create PendingIntent for broadcast
  - Intent targets EvaluationReminderReceiver
  - Includes decision ID and title as extras
  - Uses FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | FLAG_IMMUTABLE

- `toRequestCode(long)`: Convert decision ID to request code
  - Hash function: decisionId ^ (decisionId >>> 32)
  - Math.abs() to ensure positive
  - Used for PendingIntent uniqueness

### EvaluationReminderReceiver.java
Broadcast receiver that handles notification triggers.

**Key Methods**:
- `onReceive(Context, Intent)`: Handle alarm trigger
  - Creates notification channel
  - Checks POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission (Android 13+)
  - Extracts decision ID and title from intent
  - Creates notification with decision details
  - Sets pending intent to open DecisionDetailActivity
  - Shows notification

**Notification Details**:
- Small icon: android.R.drawable.ic_popup_reminder
- Auto-cancel: true
- Priority: DEFAULT
- Content intent: Opens DecisionDetailActivity with decision ID

### BootReceiver.java
Reschedules notifications after device reboot.

**Key Methods**:
- `onReceive(Context, Intent)`: Handle BOOT_COMPLETED
  - Fetches pending decisions from repository
  - Reschedules all reminders for pending decisions
  - Runs on background thread
  - Handles errors gracefully

**Purpose**: Ensures notifications persist across device reboots by rescheduling them when the device boots.

### ActivityScheduler.java
Manages periodic checks for missed activities using WorkManager.

**Key Methods**:
- `scheduleMissedActivityCheck(Context)`: Schedule periodic WorkManager task
  - Uses PeriodicWorkRequest with 15-minute interval
  - Sets minimal constraints (no battery/charging requirements)
  - Uses REPLACE policy to update existing work
  - Targets ActivityMissedWorker

- `cancelMissedActivityCheck(Context)`: Cancel periodic checks
  - Uses WorkManager.cancelUniqueWork()
  - Stops the periodic missed activity checking

**Purpose**: Automatically checks for missed activities at regular intervals to trigger pattern detection and recovery missions.

### ActivityMissedWorker.java
WorkManager worker that handles missed activity detection.

**Key Methods**:
- `doWork()`: Execute missed activity check
  - Queries ActivityRepository for missed activities
  - Triggers pattern detection if needed
  - Creates recovery missions when patterns detected
  - Returns Result.success() or Result.failure()

**Purpose**: Background task that periodically analyzes missed activities and initiates recovery mechanisms when concerning patterns are detected.

### PreMissReminderScheduler.java
Manages pre-miss reminders for activities (10 minutes before scheduled time).

**Key Methods**:
- `schedulePreMissReminder(Context, Activity)`: Schedule reminder before activity deadline
  - Uses AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP
  - Schedules for 10 minutes before scheduledTime
  - Creates PendingIntent for PreMissReminderReceiver
  - Uses setAndAllowWhileIdle for reliability

- `cancelPreMissReminder(Context, long)`: Cancel pre-miss reminder
  - Uses AlarmManager.cancel()
  - Cancels PendingIntent for specific activity ID

**Purpose**: Provides advance warning before activities are missed, giving users a chance to complete them on time.

### PreMissReminderReceiver.java
Broadcast receiver that handles pre-miss notification triggers.

**Key Methods**:
- `onReceive(Context, Intent)`: Handle pre-miss alarm trigger
  - Creates notification channel
  - Checks POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission (Android 13+)
  - Extracts activity ID and title from intent
  - Creates notification with activity details
  - Sets pending intent to open activity completion screen
  - Shows notification

**Notification Details**:
- Small icon: android.R.drawable.ic_popup_reminder
- Auto-cancel: true
- Priority: DEFAULT
- Content intent: Opens activity for completion

**Purpose**: Warns users 10 minutes before an activity deadline to prevent missed activities.

## Utilities

### DateUtils.java
Comprehensive date and time formatting utilities.

**Key Methods**:
- `formatDateTime(long)`: Format as "dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm a"
- `formatDate(long)`: Format as "dd MMM yyyy"
- `getCategoryDisplayName(String)`: Convert category to display name
  - money → "MONEY"
  - health → "HEALTH"
  - study → "STUDY"
  - personal → "PERSONAL"
  - work → "WORK"
  - relationship → "LOVE"

- `getHourOfDay(long)`: Extract hour from timestamp (0-23)
- `buildNineAmTimestamp(int, int, int)`: Build timestamp for 9 AM on given date
- `buildDecisionTimeline(Decision)`: Build timeline string
- `buildDetailMeta(Decision)`: Build detailed metadata string

- `getHourLabel(int)`: Convert hour to time bucket
  - 23-5: "Late night"
  - 5-12: "Morning"
  - 12-17: "Afternoon"
  - 17-23: "Evening"

- `formatDetailedCountdown(long)`: Format as "Xd Xh Xm Xs"
- `formatCountdown(long)`: Format as "Xd", "Xh", "Xm", "<1m"
- `getRelativeTime(long)`: Format as "X days ago", "X hours ago", "just now"
- `toCsvValue(String)`: Escape value for CSV export (handles quotes)

### ExportUtils.java
CSV export functionality for decision history.

**Key Methods**:
- `exportDecisionHistory(Context, Uri, List<DecisionRecord>)`: Export to CSV
  - Headers: Title, Description, Category, Decision Time, Evaluation Time, Outcome, Reflection, Evaluated At
  - Proper CSV escaping with quotes
  - Uses ContentResolver for file access
  - UTF-8 encoding

**CSV Format**:
```csv
"Title","Description","Category","Decision Time","Evaluation Time","Outcome","Reflection","Evaluated At"
"Buy new phone","Expensive but necessary","MONEY","15 Apr 2026, 02:30 PM","18 Apr 2026, 09:00 AM","pending","",""
```

### ThemeManager.java
Dark/light theme management with SharedPreferences.

**Key Methods**:
- `applySavedTheme(Context)`: Apply saved theme preference
  - Reads from SharedPreferences
  - Applies MODE_NIGHT_YES or MODE_NIGHT_NO
  - Called in onCreate of activities

- `toggleTheme(Context)`: Switch between dark and light mode
  - Reads current preference
  - Toggles boolean value
  - Saves to SharedPreferences
  - Applies new theme

**Storage**:
- Preferences file: "decision_graveyard_prefs"
- Key: "dark_mode"
- Default: false (light mode)

---

## Key Flows

### 1. Decision Creation Flow
```
User enters decision details in AddDecisionFragment
  ↓
User clicks "Save Decision"
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment.attemptSaveDecision()
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment.showConsequenceMomentDialog()
  ↓
User confirms in dialog
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment.performSaveWithEffect()
  ↓
Vibration feedback + fade-out animation
  ↓
AddDecisionViewModel.saveDecision()
  ↓
Validates input (title, category, future evaluation time)
  ↓
Generates unique decision ID
  ↓
Checks for similar decisions (async on ExecutorService)
  ↓
DecisionRepository.createDecision() with callback
  ↓
DecisionRepository authenticates user
  ↓
DecisionRepository creates Decision object
  ↓
DecisionRepository saves to Firestore
  ↓
DecisionRepository triggers data change notification
  ↓
AddDecisionViewModel updates SaveState LiveData
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment observes SaveState
  ↓
NotificationScheduler.scheduleReminder()
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment clears form
  ↓
Navigate to Home via bottom navigation
```

### 2. Evaluation Flow
```
User taps decision in HomeFragment
  ↓
HomeFragment.onDecisionClicked()
  ↓
Start DecisionDetailActivity with decision ID
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity.onCreate()
  ↓
DecisionDetailViewModel.loadDecision()
  ↓
DecisionRepository.getDecisionRecord()
  ↓
Fetches from Firestore
  ↓
Validates user ownership
  ↓
Builds DecisionRecord
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity observes DecisionRecord
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity.renderDecision()
  ↓
Displays decision in appropriate state (evaluated/ready/upcoming)
  ↓
User selects outcome and adds notes
  ↓
User clicks "Save evaluation"
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity.saveEvaluation()
  ↓
DecisionDetailViewModel.saveEvaluation()
  ↓
DecisionRepository.saveEvaluation()
  ↓
Updates Firestore document with outcome, reflection, timestamp
  ↓
Cancels notification for this decision
  ↓
DecisionDetailViewModel updates ActionState LiveData
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity observes ActionState
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity.refreshes decision display
```

### 3. Real-time Updates Flow
```
HomeViewModel.startListening()
  ↓
DecisionRepository.listenToDecisions()
  ↓
Firestore snapshot listener setup
  ↓
Filters by user ID
  ↓
Initial snapshot received
  ↓
Maps documents to DecisionRecord objects
  ↓
Sorts by decision time (descending)
  ↓
Callback to HomeViewModel.onChanged()
  ↓
HomeViewModel updates cached records
  ↓
HomeViewModel.postFilteredState()
  ↓
Applies status and category filters
  ↓
Updates HomeScreenState
  ↓
HomeFragment observes HomeScreenState
  ↓
HomeFragment submits list to DecisionAdapter
  ↓
RecyclerView updates UI

[Remote change occurs in Firestore]
  ↓
Firestore snapshot listener fires
  ↓
Same process repeats
  ↓
UI updates automatically
```

### 4. Analytics Calculation Flow
```
InsightsViewModel.refresh()
  ↓
DecisionRepository.getAnalyticsSnapshot()
  ↓
Fetches all DecisionRecords for user
  ↓
Iterates through records
  ↓
Counts by outcome (good/bad/neutral)
  ↓
Calculates category regret rates
  ↓
Analyzes time-of-day patterns
  ↓
Generates textual insights
  ↓
Builds AnalyticsSnapshot object
  ↓
InsightsViewModel.calculateStreak()
  ↓
Sorts records by evaluation time
  ↓
Counts consecutive good decisions within 24h
  ↓
Returns streak count
  ↓
InsightsFragment observes AnalyticsSnapshot
  ↓
InsightsFragment.renderInsights()
  ↓
Displays quality score with animation
  ↓
Shows outcome percentages
  ↓
Displays category insights with progress bars
  ↓
Shows generated insights
  ↓
InsightsFragment observes streak count
  ↓
InsightsFragment.updateStreak()
  ↓
Displays streak counter
```

### 5. Authentication Flow
```
User opens app
  ↓
LoginActivity.onCreate()
  ↓
LoginActivity.onStart()
  ↓
AuthHelper.isUserLoggedIn()
  ↓
If logged in → navigateToMain()
If not logged in → show login form
  ↓
User enters email and password
  ↓
User clicks "Login"
  ↓
LoginActivity.loginUser()
  ↓
Validates email format
  ↓
Validates password not empty
  ↓
Sets loading state
  ↓
AuthHelper.login()
  ↓
FirebaseAuth.signInWithEmailAndPassword()
  ↓
On success:
  ↓
  AuthHelper.upsertUserDocument()
  ↓
  Checks if user document exists
  ↓
  Sets createdAt for new users
  ↓
  Merges data for existing users
  ↓
  Callback to LoginActivity.onSuccess()
  ↓
  LoginActivity.navigateToMain()
  ↓
  Clears back stack
  ↓
  Starts MainActivity

On error:
  ↓
  AuthHelper.mapAuthError()
  ↓
  Converts to user-friendly message
  ↓
  Callback to LoginActivity.onError()
  ↓
  LoginActivity shows error toast
  ↓
  LoginActivity clears loading state
```

### 6. Notification Flow
```
User creates decision with evaluation time
  ↓
AddDecisionFragment observes SaveState.success
  ↓
NotificationScheduler.scheduleReminder()
  ↓
Creates AlarmManager
  ↓
Calculates trigger time (evaluation time)
  ↓
Builds PendingIntent for EvaluationReminderReceiver
  ↓
Sets alarm with setAndAllowWhileIdle()
  ↓
[Time passes]
  ↓
Alarm triggers at evaluation time
  ↓
EvaluationReminderReceiver.onReceive()
  ↓
Creates notification channel
  ↓
Checks POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission
  ↓
Builds notification with decision details
  ↓
Creates PendingIntent for DecisionDetailActivity
  ↓
Shows notification
  ↓
User taps notification
  ↓
Opens DecisionDetailActivity with decision ID
  ↓
User evaluates decision
  ↓
DecisionDetailActivity cancels notification

### 7. Activity Creation Flow
```
User enters activity details in AddActivityFragment
  ↓
User clicks "Save Activity"
  ↓
AddActivityFragment.attemptSaveActivity()
  ↓
ActivityRepository.createActivity() with callback
  ↓
ActivityRepository authenticates user
  ↓
ActivityRepository generates unique activity ID
  ↓
ActivityRepository creates Activity object
  ↓
ActivityRepository saves to Firestore
  ↓
ActivityRepository triggers data change notification
  ↓
PreMissReminderScheduler.schedulePreMissReminder()
  ↓
AddActivityFragment observes callback
  ↓
Navigate to ActivityListFragment

### 8. Pattern Detection Flow
```
ActivityMissedWorker.doWork() (runs every 15 minutes)
  ↓
ActivityRepository.detectFailurePattern()
  ↓
Fetches activities from last 7 days
  ↓
Analyzes consecutive missed activities
  ↓
Identifies most missed category
  ↓
Detects pattern if 3+ consecutive misses
  ↓
Calculates recovery activities needed
  ↓
Returns PatternTracker with analysis
  ↓
If pattern detected:
  ↓
RecoveryMissionRepository.createRecoveryMission()
  ↓
Saves recovery mission to Firestore
  ↓
Shows pattern notification to user

### 9. LLM Insights Generation Flow
```
User opens InsightsFragment
  ↓
InsightsViewModel.refresh()
  ↓
DecisionRepository.getAnalyticsSnapshot()
  ↓
Formats decision data for LLM
  ↓
LLMService.generateInsights(decisionData, brutalMode)
  ↓
Builds prompt with decision data
  ↓
Calls Gemini API (gemini-1.5-flash)
  ↓
Waits for response (async via CompletableFuture)
  ↓
On success:
  ↓
Returns generated insights
  ↓
InsightsViewModel updates LiveData
  ↓
InsightsFragment observes and displays insights

On failure:
  ↓
LLMService returns null
  ↓
InsightsViewModel uses fallback pre-defined texts
  ↓
InsightsFragment displays fallback insights

### 10. Google Sign-In Flow
```
User opens LoginActivity
  ↓
User clicks "Sign in with Google"
  ↓
LoginActivity.initiateGoogleSignIn()
  ↓
GoogleSignInClient.getSignInIntent()
  ↓
Google Sign-In intent launched
  ↓
User selects Google account
  ↓
LoginActivity.onActivityResult()
  ↓
Extracts GoogleSignInAccount from intent
  ↓
AuthHelper.signInWithGoogle(GoogleSignInAccount, callback)
  ↓
Converts Google ID token to Firebase AuthCredential
  ↓
FirebaseAuth.signInWithCredential()
  ↓
On success:
  ↓
AuthHelper.upsertUserDocument()
  ↓
Creates/updates user in Firestore
  ↓
Callback to LoginActivity.onSuccess()
  ↓
LoginActivity.navigateToMain()
  ↓
Starts MainActivity

On error:
  ↓
AuthHelper.mapAuthError()
  ↓
Converts to user-friendly message
  ↓
Callback to LoginActivity.onError()
  ↓
LoginActivity shows error toast
```

## Design Patterns

### 1. Repository Pattern
**Location**: `DecisionRepository.java`

**Purpose**: Centralized data access abstraction

**Implementation**:
- Single repository class for all decision-related data operations
- Abstracts Firebase Firestore complexity from ViewModels
- Provides both sync and async methods
- Handles authentication and user filtering internally

**Benefits**:
- Single source of truth for data operations
- Easy to swap data sources (Firestore → Room → API)
- Centralized error handling
- Testable business logic

### 2. MVVM Architecture
**Location**: All ViewModels and UI components

**Purpose**: Separation of UI and business logic

**Implementation**:
- **Model**: Data classes (Decision, Evaluation, etc.)
- **View**: Activities and Fragments
- **ViewModel**: Business logic and state management
- LiveData for reactive updates

**Benefits**:
- Testable business logic (ViewModels without UI)
- Survives configuration changes
- Clear separation of concerns
- Reactive UI updates

### 3. Observer Pattern
**Location**: LiveData throughout the app

**Purpose**: Reactive data updates

**Implementation**:
- ViewModel exposes LiveData for state
- UI observes LiveData and updates automatically
- Firestore snapshot listeners for real-time remote updates

**Benefits**:
- Automatic UI updates on data changes
- No manual refresh needed
- Lifecycle-aware (no memory leaks)

### 4. Callback Pattern
**Location**: Repository callbacks, AuthHelper callbacks

**Purpose**: Handle async operations

**Implementation**:
```java
interface ActionCallback {
    void onSuccess();
    void onError(String error);
}
```

**Benefits**:
- Handles async Firebase operations
- Provides success/error feedback
- Allows UI updates on completion

### 5. Singleton Pattern
**Location**: `AuthHelper.java`, `ThemeManager.java`

**Purpose**: Single instance for global operations

**Implementation**:
- Static methods only
- No public constructors
- Private constructors prevent instantiation

**Benefits**:
- Global access point
- Single instance ensures consistency
- Memory efficient

### 6. Builder Pattern
**Location**: `NotificationCompat.Builder`, `MaterialAlertDialogBuilder`

**Purpose**: Complex object construction

**Implementation**:
- Chainable method calls
- Fluent API
- Optional parameters

**Benefits**:
- Readable code
- Flexible object creation
- Handles complex construction logic

### 7. Factory Pattern
**Location**: `ViewModelProvider.AndroidViewModelFactory`

**Purpose**: Create ViewModel instances

**Implementation**:
- Factory creates ViewModels with Application context
- Handles ViewModel lifecycle

**Benefits**:
- Centralized creation logic
- Consistent instantiation
- Handles dependencies

### 8. Adapter Pattern
**Location**: RecyclerView adapters

**Purpose**: Adapt data for display

**Implementation**:
- `DecisionAdapter` adapts DecisionRecord to RecyclerView
- `InsightAdapter` adapts String insights to RecyclerView

**Benefits**:
- Separates data from display logic
- Reusable components
- Efficient list rendering

### 9. Strategy Pattern
**Location**: Date formatting strategies in `DateUtils`

**Purpose**: Different formatting algorithms

**Implementation**:
- Multiple format methods for different use cases
- Same input, different output based on strategy

**Benefits**:
- Flexible formatting
- Easy to add new formats
- Clear separation of strategies

---

## Data Flow Summary

### Unidirectional Data Flow
```
User Input
    ↓
Fragment/Activity
    ↓
ViewModel (Business Logic)
    ↓
Repository (Data Access)
    ↓
Firebase Firestore (Cloud Storage)
    ↓
Repository (Data Mapping)
    ↓
ViewModel (State Update)
    ↓
LiveData (Reactive Trigger)
    ↓
Fragment/Activity (UI Update)
```

### Real-time Data Flow
```
Firestore Remote Change
    ↓
Snapshot Listener
    ↓
Repository (Data Mapping)
    ↓
ViewModel (State Update)
    ↓
LiveData (Reactive Trigger)
    ↓
Fragment/Activity (UI Update)
```

### Key Principles
- **Data flows downward**: From repository to UI
- **Events flow upward**: From UI to repository
- **State is immutable**: New state objects created on changes
- **Reactive updates**: LiveData and snapshot listeners handle updates automatically
- **Single source of truth**: Repository is the only data access point

## Development Guidelines

### Adding New Features

1. **Create Model**: Add data class in `model/` package
2. **Update Repository**: Add data access methods in `DecisionRepository.java`
3. **Create ViewModel**: Add ViewModel in `viewmodel/` package if needed
4. **Update UI**: Add/modify Fragment or Activity
5. **Test**: Verify data flow and UI updates

### Code Style

- **Java 11**: Use modern Java features
- **Naming**: Follow Android naming conventions
- **Comments**: Document public methods and complex logic
- **Error Handling**: Always handle errors gracefully with user-friendly messages

### Testing Considerations

- **ViewModels**: Test business logic without UI
- **Repository**: Mock Firebase operations
- **UI**: Use Espresso for UI tests
- **Utils**: Unit test utility methods

---

## Troubleshooting

### Common Issues

**Firebase Authentication Failures**:
- Check internet connection
- Verify Firebase project configuration
- Check auth error messages in AuthHelper

**Firestore Query Errors**:
- Verify user authentication
- Check collection and document names
- Verify Firestore rules

**Notification Not Showing**:
- Check POST_NOTIFICATIONS permission (Android 13+)
- Verify notification channel creation
- Check AlarmManager permissions
- Test with different trigger times

**Analytics Not Updating**:
- Check Firebase listener registration
- Verify data change triggers
- Check filter logic in ViewModel

DecisionGraveyard is a well-architected Android application that demonstrates modern Android development practices including MVVM architecture, Firebase integration, reactive programming with LiveData, and Material Design. The codebase is organized with clear separation of concerns and follows established design patterns for maintainability and scalability.

The application successfully combines:
- **Cloud storage** with Firebase Firestore
- **Real-time updates** with snapshot listeners

This documentation provides a comprehensive overview of the entire codebase, making it easy for developers to understand, maintain, and extend the application.

rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    match /users/{userId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
    }

    match /userProfiles/{userId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && request.auth.uid == userId
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;
    }

    match /decisions/{docId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;
    }

    match /activities/{docId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;

    match /recovery_missions/{docId} {

      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()

      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;
    }

    match /causeEffectInsights/{docId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;
    }

    match /userProgress/{userId} {
      allow read, write: if isOwner(userId);
    }

    match /dailyCheckIns/{docId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;
    }

    match /analytics/{docId} {
      allow create: if isSignedIn()
        && request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow read, delete: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
      allow update: if isSignedIn()

      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId;

# DecisionGraveyard - Full Project Reference

## 1) Project Summary

DecisionGraveyard is a Java Android app that helps users make better long-term decisions through delayed review, reflection, behavior tracking, and pattern-aware coaching.

The product combines:
- Decision journaling with delayed judgment
- Activity planning and execution tracking
- Pattern detection (miss streaks, risky decision windows)
- AI-generated insights and actionable recommendations
- Gamified profile progress (XP/levels/streaks)
- Notification systems for reminders and behavior nudges

Architecture style is **MVVM + Repository + Firebase-backed realtime data** with Android Fragments/Activities on top.

---

## 2) Core Features

- **Auth & Identity**
  - Email/password login + registration
  - Google Sign-In support
  - Per-user data isolation via Firebase Auth UID

- **Decision Lifecycle**
  - Create decision with category and future review time
  - Duplicate/similarity checks
  - Risk warnings based on historical regret patterns
  - Evaluate outcome (`good` / `bad` / `neutral`) with notes
  - Track pending/ready/reviewed states

- **Activities & Discipline**
  - Schedule activities by category/time
  - Mark complete or detect missed
  - Pre-miss reminders before deadline
  - Detect repeated misses and generate recovery missions

- **Dashboard / Insights / Timeline**
  - Aggregated metrics and trend views
  - Category regret insights + time-of-day analysis
  - Cause-effect style insights linking activity misses and decision quality
  - Timeline visualization of behavior history

- **AI Layer**
  - Gemini-powered textual insights via `LLMService`
  - Fallback deterministic insights when LLM is unavailable

- **Behavior & Notifications**
  - Decision review reminders
  - Activity pre-miss reminders
  - Daily/weekly smart notifications and behavioral checks via WorkManager

---

## 3) Tech Stack

- **Platform**: Android (minSdk 24, targetSdk 36, compileSdk 36)
- **Language**: Java 11
- **Architecture**: MVVM + Repository pattern + LiveData
- **Backend**: Firebase
  - Firebase Authentication
  - Cloud Firestore
  - Firestore Security Rules
- **AI**: Google Generative AI SDK (`gemini-1.5-flash`) via `LLMService`
- **Background Jobs**: WorkManager
- **Notifications**: AlarmManager + BroadcastReceivers + Notification channels
- **UI**: AndroidX + Material Components + RecyclerView
- **Build**: Gradle Kotlin DSL + Google Services plugin
- **Testing**: JUnit + AndroidX test dependencies

---

## 4) High-Level Component Interaction

1. UI (Fragment/Activity) captures user action.
2. ViewModel validates/transforms state.
3. Repository executes Firestore/logic operations.
4. Repository emits updates via LiveData/callback/snapshot listener.
5. ViewModel maps repository results to UI state.
6. UI re-renders.

Cross-cutting flows:
- **AuthHelper** gates authenticated access.
- **RepositoryProvider** wires repository dependencies.
- **Schedulers/Workers/Receivers** trigger reminders and behavior checks.
- **LLMService** enriches analytics with generated language insights.

---

## 5) Folder Structure

```text
DecisionGraveyard/
├── app/
│   ├── src/main/java/com/sai/decisiongraveyard/
│   │   ├── MainActivity.java
│   │   ├── DecisionDetailActivity.java
│   │   ├── adapter/                  # RecyclerView adapters
│   │   ├── firebase/                 # AuthHelper
│   │   ├── model/                    # Domain models
│   │   ├── notifications/            # Schedulers, workers, receivers
│   │   ├── repository/               # Data/business repositories
│   │   ├── service/                  # Legacy/manual LLM connector
│   │   ├── ui/                       # Feature fragments/activities/widgets
│   │   ├── util/                     # Utilities
│   │   └── viewmodel/                # MVVM view models
│   ├── src/main/res/                 # Layouts, drawables, menus, values
│   └── src/test/                     # Unit tests
├── firestore.rules
├── TEST_CASES.md
├── DOCUMENTATION.md
├── build.gradle.kts
├── settings.gradle.kts
└── gradle/ (libs.versions.toml, wrapper config)
```

---

## 6) Key Runtime Modules

- **Bootstrap**
  - `MainActivity`: root navigation, permissions, scheduling startup
  - `RepositoryProvider`: singleton dependency wiring

- **Repositories**
  - `DecisionRepository`
  - `ActivityRepository`
  - `RecoveryMissionRepository`
  - `UserProfileRepository`
  - `UserPreferencesRepository`
  - `DailyCheckInRepository`
  - `CauseEffectRepository`
  - `TimelineRepository`
  - `AnalyticsRepository`

- **Main UI Areas**
  - Dashboard: `DashboardContainerFragment`, `DashboardFragment`
  - Decisions: `HomeFragment`, `AddDecisionFragment`, `DecisionDetailActivity`
  - Activities: `ActivityListFragment`, `AddActivityFragment`
  - Insights: `InsightsFragment`, `AIAnalysisFragment`
  - Timeline: `TimelineFragment`
  - Profile/Settings: `ProfileFragment`, `SettingsFragment`

---

## 7) API Endpoints and External Interfaces

This app is primarily Firebase SDK-driven, so there is no internal REST backend. "Endpoints" are external services + Firestore collections.

### A) External HTTP Endpoint (legacy/manual service)

- `https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions`
  - Defined in: `service/LLMAnalysisService.java`
  - Status: appears legacy/demo (contains placeholder API key)
  - Not the main production insight path

### B) Primary AI Interface (SDK-based)

- Google Generative AI model:
  - Model name: `gemini-1.5-flash`
  - Config key source: `BuildConfig.GEMINI_API_KEY` (from `gradle.properties`)
  - Used via: `repository/LLMService.java`

### C) Firebase Authentication Interfaces

- Email/password login/register via FirebaseAuth
- Google Sign-In -> Firebase credential sign-in
- Auth orchestration in `firebase/AuthHelper.java`

### D) Firestore Data Interfaces (Collections)

- `users`
- `userProfiles`
- `decisions`
- `activities`
- `recovery_missions`
- `causeEffectInsights`
- `dailyCheckIns`
- `analytics`
- `timeline`
- `checkins`
- `userPreferences`
- `userProgress`

All reads/writes are user-scoped by `userId` and enforced by `firestore.rules`.

---

## 8) Data Schema (Firestore-Oriented)

Below is the practical schema as inferred from model + repository usage.

### `decisions` (doc ID = decisionId string)
- `id: long`
- `title: string`
- `description: string`
- `category: string`
- `decisionTime: long` (epoch ms)
- `evaluationTime: long` (epoch ms)
- `decisionHour: int`
- `userId: string`
- `outcome: string|null` (`good|bad|neutral`)
- `reflectionNotes: string`
- `evaluatedAt: long|null`

### `activities` (doc ID = activityId string)
- `activityId: long`
- `title: string`
- `category: string`
- `scheduledTime: long`
- `completed: boolean`
- `completedTime: long|null`
- `userId: string`
- `createdAt: long`

### `recovery_missions`
- `missionId: string`
- `title: string`
- `description: string`
- `activitiesToComplete: int`
- `activitiesCompleted: int`
- `deadline: long`
- `isCompleted: boolean`
- `userId: string`

### `userProfiles`
- `userId: string`
- progression/streak/XP counters (as updated by `UserProfileRepository`)

### `userPreferences`
- `userId: string`
- notification mode / goals / personalization settings

### `dailyCheckIns`
- `checkInId: string`
- `userId: string`
- daily reflection/check-in fields

### `causeEffectInsights`, `analytics`, `timeline`, `checkins`, `userProgress`
- Additional derived/behavioral/user-state collections used by specialized repositories/features.

---

## 9) Security Model (Firestore Rules)

`firestore.rules` enforces:
- Only authenticated users can read/write protected data.
- Users can only access documents where `userId == request.auth.uid`.
- User-scoped collections (`userPreferences`, `userProgress`) are tied to UID path ownership.

This prevents cross-user data access at the datastore layer.

---

## 10) Setup Instructions

## Prerequisites
- Android Studio (latest stable)
- JDK 11
- Firebase project configured for Android app
- Google Services config file in app module (`google-services.json`)

## Local Setup
1. Open project in Android Studio.
2. Ensure `gradle.properties` contains:
   - `gemini.api.key=<YOUR_GEMINI_API_KEY>`
3. Ensure Firebase is configured:
   - Auth providers enabled (email/password, Google if required)
   - Firestore database created
   - Firestore rules applied from `firestore.rules`
4. Sync Gradle.
5. Build and run app on emulator/device.

## Build/Test Commands

From project root:

```bash
./gradlew assembleDebug
./gradlew test
```

---

## 11) Main User Flows

- **Auth flow**: Login/Register -> user document upsert -> main app
- **Decision flow**: Add decision -> schedule reminder -> review later -> evaluate -> analytics refresh
- **Activity flow**: Add activity -> pre-miss reminder -> complete/miss -> pattern detection
- **Recovery flow**: repeated misses -> mission creation -> completion tracking
- **Insights flow**: aggregate decisions/activities -> generate deterministic + LLM insights

---

## 12) Important Notes and Current Reality

- Project still contains legacy/demo docs and services; current runtime architecture is richer than early docs.
- `LLMAnalysisService` is legacy/manual; `repository/LLMService` is the actively integrated path.
- Firestore indexes are intentionally minimized in some queries by fetching user-scoped docs then filtering in memory.

---

## 13) Recommended Next Improvements

- Add/refresh top-level `README.md` for contributors.
- Add explicit schema/versioning docs for Firestore collections.
- Add instrumentation tests around notification and receiver flows.
- Add CI for `assembleDebug + test` on pull requests.
- Move any remaining placeholder API-key flows out of code.

# Decision Graveyard Test Cases

## Scope

This document covers manual functional test cases for the Android app:

- Authentication
- Dashboard
- Decisions
- Activities
- Timeline
- Insights
- Profile
- Daily check-in
- Notifications
- Recovery system
- Settings
- Error handling and edge cases

Environment assumptions:

- Firebase Authentication is configured
- Firestore is connected
- Test device is Android 8+ and notifications are enabled where needed
- A clean test account is available

## Test Data

Suggested test user data:

- Email: `qa.decisiongraveyard@example.com`
- Password: `Qa@123456`

- Money
- Health
- Study
- Personal
- Work
- Relationship

Suggested activity categories:

## Authentication

### AUTH-001 Register with valid email and password

- Preconditions: User is logged out
- Steps:
  1. Open app
  2. Tap `Register`
  3. Enter valid email
  4. Enter valid password
  5. Confirm password
  6. Tap `Create account`
- Expected:
  - Account is created successfully
  - User is redirected to the main app
  - User document is created in Firestore

### AUTH-002 Register with invalid email

- Preconditions: User is logged out
- Steps:
  1. Open register screen
  2. Enter invalid email format
  3. Enter valid passwords
  4. Tap `Create account`
- Expected:
  - Email field shows validation error
  - Account is not created

### AUTH-003 Register with short password

- Preconditions: User is logged out
- Steps:
  1. Open register screen
  2. Enter valid email
  3. Enter password shorter than 6 characters
  4. Tap `Create account`
- Expected:
  - Password validation error is shown
  - Account is not created

### AUTH-004 Register with mismatched confirm password

- Preconditions: User is logged out
- Steps:
  1. Open register screen
  2. Enter valid email
  3. Enter password
  4. Enter different confirm password
  5. Tap `Create account`
- Expected:
  - Confirm password error is shown
  - Account is not created

### AUTH-005 Login with valid email/password

- Preconditions: Account exists
- Steps:
  1. Open login screen
  2. Enter valid credentials
  3. Tap `Login`
- Expected:
  - Login succeeds
  - User lands on main screen

### AUTH-006 Login with wrong password

- Preconditions: Account exists
- Steps:
  1. Enter valid email
  2. Enter wrong password
  3. Tap `Login`
- Expected:
  - Error message is shown
  - User remains on login screen

### AUTH-007 Reset password with valid email

- Preconditions: Account exists
- Steps:
  1. Enter registered email on login screen
  2. Tap `Forgot password`
- Expected:
  - Reset email is sent
  - Confirmation toast/message is shown

### AUTH-008 Google sign-in success

- Preconditions: Google sign-in is configured
- Steps:
  1. Tap `Sign in with Google`
  2. Select valid Google account
- Expected:
  - User is authenticated
  - User is redirected to main screen
  - Firestore user document exists
